&+\frac{1}{216}\rho^{-3}(\gamma{}^{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}){}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}(\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}){}_{\gamma}{}_{\delta}\big(\mathcal D_{a}\rho\big)T{}_{d}{}_{e}T{}_{b}{}_{c}\lambda{}^{\delta}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{i} -\frac{i}{36}\rho^{-3}(\gamma{}^{a}{}^{b}){}_{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big(\mathcal D^{c}\rho\big)T{}_{a}{}_{b}\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{i}\big(\mathcal D_{c}\lambda{}^{\alpha}{}^{i}\big)\\
&-\frac{7}{72}\rho^{-3}(\gamma{}_{a}){}^{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}(\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}){}_{\alpha}{}_{\delta}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\rho\big)T{}_{d}{}_{e}T{}_{b}{}_{c}\lambda{}^{\delta}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\gamma}{}^{i} +\frac{7}{216}\rho^{-3}(\gamma{}_{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}(\gamma{}^{b}{}^{c}){}^{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}(\gamma{}^{d}{}^{e}){}_{\gamma}{}_{\delta}\big(\mathcal D^{a}\rho\big)T{}_{d}{}_{e}T{}_{b}{}_{c}\lambda{}^{\delta}{}_{i}\lambda{}_{\beta}{}^{i}

\tag{S6-N-756}
